For task specification, we further propose the Visual Transition Token(VTT), a language-free task representation that encodes each task as a direction in visual feature space. Experiments on RoboTwin~2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot tasks demonstrate LiLa-WAM's effectiveness, achieving 90.48\% success across 50 RoboTwin tasks with single-GPU training.

</details>


### [250] [Track4Action: Distilling World-Centric 3D Tracker into Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03727)
*Chenyi Wang,Xinkai Wang,Bokai Lin,Jialin Tian,Fucheng Zhang,Cewu Lu,Lixin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Track4Action框架，通过将3D追踪器的特征蒸馏为VLA策略的对齐监督，实现更精确的机器人动作学习，显著提升了模拟与真实环境任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有vision-language-action(VLA)策略通过action label指导模仿，但缺乏如何影响3D世界的监督信息，导致机器人任务理解与泛化能力受限。作者希望利用演示视频中的丰富3D时空信息监督VLA策略对复杂场景的理解和决策。

Method: 引入Track4Action框架：训练时，用冻结的3D追踪器对演示片段提取总体特征，设计可学习的track queries，将该特征与VLA隐藏状态匹配，并用于软门控条件下的动作输出。只在训练时用追踪特征作为对齐目标，部署时无须追踪器或全片段信息。

Result: Track4Action在零样本LIBERO-Plus任务上达到82.3%，比无对齐方法提升7.6百分点，比LaMP方法高3个百分点；RoboTwin 2.0测试集分别为80.44%和81.48%；四项实际机器人任务平均成功率为67.5%，远超无对齐基线。

Conclusion: 通过3D追踪器特征赋予VLA训练'特权监督'，Track4Action在模拟与现实任务均取得可观提升，验证了3D对齐特征对机器人泛化和部署的有效性。

Abstract: Action labels tell a vision-language-action (VLA) policy which robot commands to imitate, but not how those commands change the 3D world. The aligned demonstration clip contains this missing supervision because its $K$ frame transitions record the geometry, motion, visibility, and camera change produced during the corresponding $K$ actions. We introduce Track4Action, a framework that distills this realized transition from a frozen world-centric 3D tracker into a current-observation VLA policy. During training, Track4World encodes the clip $V_{t:t+K}$ into a pooled tracker feature. Learnable track queries infer this feature from current VLA hidden states, match it in a shared space, and condition a flow-matching action head through a feature-wise gate. The tracker feature only defines the alignment target, so neither the clip nor the tracker is used at deployment. Track4Action reaches 82.3% on zero-shot LIBERO-Plus, improving the alignment-free variant by 7.6 points and LaMP by 3.0 points. It obtains 80.44% and 81.48% on the clean and randomized RoboTwin 2.0 splits, and 67.5% average success across four physical bimanual tasks, 25.0 points above the alignment-free variant. The gains across simulation and physical tasks support action-aligned 3D tracker features as privileged supervision for tracker-free VLA deployment. Our project page is available at https://wing0night.github.io/track4action-project-page.

</details>


### [251] [GORDON: Graph-based Object-centric Rewards for Decomposition of Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03753)
*Andrea Protopapa,Davide Buoso,Francesca Pistilli,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的物体中心奖励学习框架GORDON，能从无动作的视频演示中学习密集奖励，实现长期任务的自动分解，并显著提升RL算法在多步操作任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强学习在长期操作任务中面临奖励设计难、稀疏奖励指导性弱、手工子任务划分成本高等问题。虽然视觉演示能辅助奖励学习，来自像素的奖励对视觉变化、背景等较为敏感和脆弱，需要更鲁棒的奖励表示方法。

Method: 每帧视觉场景用检测到的物体与空间关系构建图表达。使用自监督训练的图神经网络（GNN）将物体图编码为对齐任务进展的潜在空间。引入活动感知加权池化，突出与任务相关的物体、抑制机器人主导的运动。以当前状态与目标配置的潜在空间距离计算密集奖励。长期任务中，奖励的时间分布反映分阶段的物体状态转变，实现自动子任务分段，并基于此训练子任务奖励与策略，最后顺序组合子策略。

Result: 在MAGICAL和ManiSkill3基准的七个操作任务上，所提出的奖励体系提升了短期RL训练效果，并能自动分解复杂长期任务，使平均成功率达到74.4%，比最佳学习基线高约35个百分点，比oracle高约25个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于图的物体中心密集奖励能够从无动作视频演示中有效学习，兼具奖励鲁棒性及子任务自动发现，极大提升了复杂长期操作任务中的RL训练效率与成功率。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon manipulation skills with reinforcement learning remains challenging due to the complexity of reward design, the limited guidance of sparse rewards, and the high cost of manual subtask annotation. Visual demonstrations can provide supervision for reward learning, but rewards learned from raw pixels can be brittle and sensitive to visual variation, background appearance, and robot motion. In this work, we propose GORDON, a graph-based object-centric reward learning framework that learns dense rewards from action-free video demonstrations. Each visual scene is represented as a graph of detected objects and spatial relations, and a graph neural network is trained in a self-supervised manner to embed these graphs into a task-aligned latent space. To align the representation with semantic task progress, we introduce an activity-aware weighted pooling mechanism that emphasizes task-relevant objects while masking robot-dominated motion. The dense reward is then computed as distances in the learned latent space of the current state to demonstrated goal configurations, providing a measure of task progress. In long-horizon tasks, the temporal profile of this reward reveals stage-wise object-state transitions, enabling automatic subtask discovery without manual segmentation. The discovered segments are then used to train subtask-specific rewards and specialized policies that are composed sequentially. Experiments on seven manipulation tasks on MAGICAL and ManiSkill3 benchmarks show that our object-centric reward improves reinforcement learning in short-horizon settings and enables successful policy learning in complex long-horizon tasks through automatic decomposition, achieving an average success rate of 74.4% across the long-horizon tasks (on average approximately +35 p.p. vs. best learned baseline and approximately +25 p.p. vs. oracle).

</details>


### [252] [Design and Evaluation of an AI-Enabled Cloud-Edge Architecture for Connected Precision Agriculture Farms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03816)
*Deckshitha Angadi,Koteshwar Goud Surga,Nagaraju Lakkaraju,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Giridhara Venkata Ram Raj Mulasa,Ravi Killamsetty,Chandrasekhara Sarma Mallubhotla,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用AI的云-边协同架构，实现对番茄常见病害的自动化监测，在不同平台上病害识别准确率达92-95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 番茄等农作物的病害会造成巨大产量损失，传统人工监测难以适应大规模农场管理，因此亟需高效、自动化的技术手段提升病害检测和响应效率。

Method: 结合物联网传感器、无人机、深度学习和Azure云服务，设计了一个跨移动端、Web端和边缘设备的智能系统。利用PlantVillage和Kaggle等公开数据集训练TensorFlow模型，并部署到不同的应用平台，实现实时、自动的番茄病害检测。

Result: 系统在多种环境和不同设备平台上病害识别准确率为92-95%，表现稳定、效果良好，实现了多平台间的有效协同。

Conclusion: 所提系统可提升农作物病害检测效率，减少对人工依赖，实现作物种植的智能化和可持续发展，对促进精准农业具有重要意义。

Abstract: Plant diseases cause significant yield losses worldwide, with tomato crops particularly susceptible to early blight, late blight, and leaf mold. Manual monitoring is practical only for small-scale farms and becomes unmanageable at larger scales. To tackle this limitation, an artificial intelligence (AI) enabled cloud-edge architecture is proposed for autonomous crop monitoring. This proposed architecture integrates Internet of Things (IoT) sensors, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), deep learning, Azure IoT Hub-based cloud analytics, and multi-platform (mobile app, web app, and embedded edge device platform) interfaces to enable real-time detection of tomato diseases. For training and validation, we used publicly available datasets, such as PlantVillage and Kaggle. A TensorFlow model trained on a collected dataset is deployed across mobile, web, and edge-device platforms. Experimental results show detection effectiveness around 92-95%, with consistent performance over diverse environments and device platforms. The proposed system improves disease detection effectiveness, lowers dependence on manual inspection, and enables prompt interventions, thereby supporting sustainable, connected precision agriculture farms.

</details>


### [253] [Designing Social Robots for Inclusive Child Wellbeing Assessment: Insights from Communities Supporting Developmental Language Disorder and Forced Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03820)
*Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Yue Lou,Alva Markelius,Emma Geijer-Simpson,Gustaf Gredebäck,Tamsin Jane Ford,Ginevra Castellano,Hatice Gunes,Georgina Warner,Jenny L. Gibson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用社交机器人辅助评估有沟通障碍儿童（包括语言发育障碍和强制移民背景儿童）的幸福感和心理健康，并提出了相应的包容性和伦理性设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自我报告问卷在评估儿童幸福感和心理健康时对语言理解和表达有较高要求，限制了沟通障碍儿童的有效参与。当前关于社交机器人如何包容这些儿童的研究有限，因此需要探索新的评估及其设计方法。

Method: 研究者设计了候选的儿童与机器人互动活动作为设计探针，并组织家长和专业人士（服务于目标儿童群体）焦点小组，借助主题分析法识别了机器人角色、互动动态、个体差异、儿童主体性等相关考量。

Result: 发现了影响机器人中介幸福感评估的多方面重要因素，包括针对不同沟通需求和生活经历的人群特异性设计，归纳出一套伦理且包容的设计建议。

Conclusion: 本文为如何基于机器人技术进行面向多样沟通需求儿童的包容性幸福感评估提供了设计指导，有助于未来开发更包容、适宜和伦理的机器人辅助评估工具。

Abstract: Assessing children's wellbeing and mental health can be particularly challenging for children experiencing communication barriers, such as children with Developmental Language Disorder (DLD) and children with forced migration backgrounds. During the assessment process, traditional self-report questionnaires place substantial demands on language comprehension and verbal expression. In this context, social robots have emerged as a promising tool for supporting wellbeing assessment without solely relying on self-report questionnaires, yet limited research has examined how such interactions can be designed to be inclusive, appropriate, and ethically acceptable for children with diverse communication needs. To address this gap, we created candidate child--robot interaction activities as design probes and conducted focus groups with parents and professionals supporting children with DLD and children with forced migration backgrounds. Through thematic analysis, we identified considerations relating to robot role and capabilities, interactional dynamics, individual differences, and child agency, alongside population-specific considerations shaped by children's communication needs and lived experiences. Based on these findings, we derive a set of ethical and inclusive design recommendations for robot-mediated wellbeing assessment. By foregrounding these considerations and recommendations, this work contributes design guidance for inclusive robot-mediated wellbeing assessments for children with diverse communication needs.

</details>


### [254] [EvoHIL: Self-Evolving Reward and Flow-Matched Policy Optimization for Robust Human-in-the-Loop Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03872)
*Shuoqin Zhang,Tongtong Cheng,Xiru Gao,Jinzhuo Peng,Bin Zheng,Jiahao Tu,Ke Wang,Jia Pan,Zhe Hu,Kai Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EvoHIL框架，解决人机交互强化学习中视觉奖励失效、动作不连贯和视觉敏感性等问题。EvoHIL在多项实际操作任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前人机交互强化学习在机器人操作中面临视觉奖励对环境变化不鲁棒、动作采样引发的不连贯性，以及视觉策略对外观变化敏感等限制。该文旨在提出能够动态适应环境变化、提升动作流平滑性并增强视觉策略鲁棒性的统一方法。

Method: EvoHIL框架包含三部分：1）自进化奖励（SER），结合人类反馈动态调整成功判别器；2）动作流稳定化（AFS），通过流匹配生成时序连贯的动作片段，基于历史已执行动作和演示进行策略更新；3）保留感知的离线微调，利用重新光照处理的数据微调视觉域，并保持AFS策略的行为一致性，无需额外真人机器人交互。

Result: 在Franka FR3与SO-101机械臂的六种操作任务和受控光照变化环境下，EvoHIL相比传统人机交互和模仿学习基线，能提升任务成功率、与人类评判的一致性、动作平滑度及任务完成效率。

Conclusion: EvoHIL作为一种统一的人机交互强化学习框架，解决了奖励、动作和视觉端的核心瓶颈，并在多项实际操作任务中取得卓越效果，展现了强大实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Human-in-the-loop reinforcement learning (HIL-RL) enables robots to learn contact-rich manipulation from limited real-world interaction, but deployment exposes three coupled limitations: static visual reward models fail under scene changes; independently sampled actions cause temporally inconsistent motion; and vision-based policies remain sensitive to appearance shifts. We present EvoHIL, a unified framework that adapts the reward model, action generator, and visual do main within a staged human-in-the-loop learning process. First, self-evolving reward (SER) adapts the success classifier from human-confirmed positives and provisional weak negatives. Second, Action Flow Stabilization (AFS) generates temporally coherent action chunks through flow matching, grounding policy updates in executed action prefixes and demonstrated behavior. Third, retention-aware offline fine-tuning replays relit interaction data while anchoring the AFS actor-critic to prior behavior, adapting the visual domain without additional robot interaction. Across six manipulation tasks on Franka FR3 and SO-101 arms under a controlled lighting shift, EvoHIL improves task success, agreement with human-confirmation labels, motion smoothness, and completion time relative to human-in-the-loop and imitation baselines.Project page: https://anonymous4366.github.io/EvoHIL/

</details>


### [255] [ETA: A New Agentic Paradigm for Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03924)
*Yitong Chen,Zezheng Huai,Sixian Li,Yubang Wang,Haozhe Zhang,Yifei Zhang,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文引入了Embodied Task Agent（ETA），提出了一种推广数码智能体到物理世界的新范式，并开源了其实现OpenETA。ETA通过分离规划、操作和环境反馈，使机器人能更灵活适应新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人系统在实际环境中的泛化和长期控制能力有限，难以比肩ChatGPT在语言领域的突破。作者希望通过新架构让机器人具备更强的适应性和可控性，推动“机器人ChatGPT时刻”的到来。

Method: 提出ETA架构，将传统的端到端观测到动作模式拆分为规划器（Planner）、工具与技能（Tools/Skills）、可审核的记忆、可重播的轨迹，以及通用接口，适用于仿真和真实机器人。实现了开源版本OpenETA，支持插件式调用和灵活管理各种模块。

Result: OpenETA能够让数字化智能体延伸至物理机器人，其架构易于模块更换与组装，支持操作审核和轨迹重放，能够适配如Codex等智能体作为轻量插件，提升了机器人系统的泛化、可靠性与复用性。

Conclusion: ETA与OpenETA为机器人实现像ChatGPT那样的泛化和学习能力提供了方法学基础，通过可插拔和可审核的新架构提升了任务执行能力，为实现通用机器人向前迈进一步。

Abstract: When will robots have their ChatGPT moment? Such a breakthrough requires a general-purpose robot that can handle unfamiliar tasks in unfamiliar environments, remain controllable over long interactions, and learn from experience.
  Today's embodied systems largely follow an end-to-end observation-to-action path. Despite rapid progress, they remain far from this goal: their generalization depends heavily on the coverage of robot training data, while long task execution remains difficult to control and inspect. To realize this goal, we introduce the Embodied Task Agent (ETA), a new paradigm for extending digital agents into the physical world, and release OpenETA as its open-source implementation. ETA centers the robot around a Planner that chooses one Tool call at a time, an Interface that controls execution, and a World that returns the result and a fresh observation. This loop allows the agent to verify outcomes, adapt its plan, and turn successful and failed interactions into reusable experience. OpenETA provides replaceable Planners, composable Tools and Skills, auditable memory, replayable trajectories, and common interfaces for simulation and real robots. For Codex, OpenETA can operate as a lightweight plugin that exposes only observe, mark_point, and move_to.

</details>


### [256] [Bimanual Manipulation Within an 8 GB Budget: Zero-Copy Sensing and Quantized ACT on an Entry-Level Jetson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03938)
*Ekansh Singh,Eva Samuel,Alessandra Reneau,Ryan Schmeelk,Yashvi Gandhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了在入门级嵌入式硬件（NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super）上部署模仿学习训练的双臂机器人操作策略的可行性，分析了传感、策略训练与推理加速等主要环节，并提出一系列优化方案，验证了在低成本硬件上仍可实现高性能操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前双臂机器人操作策略大多在高性能GPU上训练与测试，缺乏在嵌入式低功耗硬件上实际部署和性能评估的数据。随着机器人应用向成本敏感和移动场景发展，证明低成本硬件的部署可行性变得非常重要。

Method: 1. 在Jetson Orin Nano Super上搭建三摄像头实时采集系统，优化传感数据流以减少CPU瓶颈并降低延迟；2. 使用桌面GPU离线训练ACT与Diffusion Policy两种模仿学习方法，并对比在推理速度和精度上的表现；3. 将ACT模型转为TensorRT，分别使用FP16与INT8量化，比对精度与推理延迟的变化；4. 深入分析TensorRT INT8量化在ResNet18与transformer架构上的适用性及其对模型尺寸和速度的影响。

Result: 1. 零拷贝感知优化显著降低CPU压力和延迟；2. ACT能成功收敛并实现有效操作，而Diffusion Policy未能在本实验收敛，显示出两者对迭代次数的不同需求；3. ACT经FP16和INT8量化后，推理时间大幅下降，同时维持高任务成功率；4. INT8量化对transformer有限，仅ResNet部分被量化，实测模型体积减小有限但速度提升明显。

Conclusion: 1. 入门级嵌入式设备可胜任高精度双臂操作策略的实时部署。2. 算法和感知管线的硬件适配对系统性能影响显著。3. ACT在有限步数训练和推理压缩方面相比Diffusion Policy更适合此次嵌入式部署场景。4. 对模型架构细节（如transformer量化限制）需进一步研究和优化。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation policies trained with imitation learning are typically evaluated on workstation or datacenter-class GPUs, leaving the cost of deploying them on embedded hardware largely uncharacterized. We present a bimanual SO-101 system running entirely on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano Super (8 GB), the entry-level tier of NVIDIA's embedded line, using a desktop GPU (RTX 3070) only for offline training, evaluated on pick-and-place of a deformable beanbag. First, we build a GStreamer capture pipeline backed by NVMM buffers that removes redundant host-device copies from three-camera sensing. Contrary to expectation, the conventional path fit the memory budget and dropped no frames; what zero-copy sensing recovers is CPU headroom (peak single-core utilization 98.0% to 77.0%) and worst-case latency (117.31 ms to 101.52 ms). Second, we train ACT and Diffusion Policy on identical demonstrations, each at its own reference budget (100k gradient steps for ACT, 200k for Diffusion Policy). ACT converges to a task-competent policy (19/20 trials) while Diffusion Policy does not converge to a usable one (0/10) even at twice the step count, which we attribute to differing convergence costs rather than an accuracy ceiling. Third, we convert ACT to TensorRT. FP16 reduces mean inference latency from 114.02 ms to 17.93 ms (6.4x) and INT8 to 12.65 ms (9.0x), with task success preserved at all three precisions (19/20, 18/20, 19/20). We report two findings not previously documented for ACT: TensorRT's general-purpose INT8 calibration quantizes the ResNet18 backbone but accepts zero of 145 transformer layers, explaining INT8's negligible size reduction over FP16 (0.9%) despite a further 28% latency gain; and the need for quantization is conditional on ACT's action-chunking configuration, feasible in full precision at n_action_steps = 100 but not at the per-step re-prediction temporal ensembling requires.

</details>


### [257] [Stochastic Multiple Shooting Trajectory Optimization via Sequential Local Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03978)
*Ashwin Gupta,Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的随机多点射击（multiple shooting）轨迹优化方法，通过优化相连的短时间控制序列，利用局部反馈策略更高效地满足终端约束条件。该方法还能仅凭rollout数据近似系统雅可比矩阵，适用于黑箱动力学的模型强化学习，并在多个非线性动力学任务上展示了更优的样本效率和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机单点射击轨迹优化方法虽兼容概率动力学、适应梯度不可得场景，但在长时序和终端约束下极其样本低效，导致收敛慢。在难以获取显式模型的强化学习场景下，这一问题更为突出。

Method: 作者提出了随机多点射击方法，将长控制序列分解为若干短区段，各区段通过局部反馈策略连接。方法还可以通过系统rollout反推近似雅可比矩阵，无须模型显式表达。

Result: 在三个非线性动力学任务（分析动力学下的cartpole swingup，神经网络建模动力学下的cartpole，和高性能VTOL quadplane着陆任务）上，算法展现了比传统方法更好的样本效率与终端收敛性。

Conclusion: 新方法不仅提升了终端约束收敛效率，还适应了黑箱动力学环境，对复杂强化学习任务有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Stochastic single shooting trajectory optimization methods such as Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) have been widely adopted in robotics due to their ability to reason about probabilistic dynamics and provide solutions where model gradients are noisy, costly to evaluate, or unavailable. However, satisfaction of terminal constraints when shooting over long action sequences is often sample inefficient, requiring a large number of iterations for convergence. In this paper, we present a stochastic multiple shooting method that optimizes short control action sequences connected via local feedback policies to improve sample efficiency and convergence to a terminal set. Additionally, we show that we are able to synthesize approximate system Jacobians purely from rollouts, making the method suitable for model-based reinforcement learning with black-box dynamics. We demonstrate the algorithm has improved sample efficiency and terminal set convergence for three nonlinear, underactuated optimization problems: a classic cartpole swingup task with analytical dynamics, a cartpole swingup task with learned neural network dynamics, and a VTOL quadplane performing a high angle-of-attack, precision post-stall landing maneuver.

</details>


### [258] [Enfold: Folding World Model Imagination into Predictive Representations for Ultra-Efficient Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26657)
*Weili Zeng,Yitong Xing,Fulong Liu,Chengqun Yang,Antao Xiang,Feng Tian,Jingnan Gao,Jisong Cai,Xin Wang,Xiaomin Wu,Yao Mu,Xiaokang Yang,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Enfold方法，将世界生成模型的未来生成计算压缩进仅依赖当前视觉和语言信息的表征中，实现无需每步调用生成器即可高效预测未来行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界生成模型往往侧重于直接生成场景或为后续模块提供复杂的上下文，但这些方法需要高昂实时计算成本，且未充分利用生成模型内部的推理过程。如何在保持高性能的同时，大幅降低生成推理的延迟和计算消耗，是机器人等实际应用场景的核心需求。

Method: Enfold方法在训练阶段，利用世界生成器转化未来轨迹过程中的多层中间状态对仅依赖当前观测的编码器进行监督训练。编码器学习提炼出能代表生成器未来推理本质的表征，该表征既可用于后续未来生成，也可直接用于任务预测，实现端到端高效决策。部署阶段，不再执行高昂的生成模型，而直接用编码器输出进行动作推理。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0和真实机器人任务上，Enfold方法实现强控制能力，并显著降低动作预测延迟——相较已有方法Fast-WAM快3.7倍，Enfold-Flash甚至达10.1倍。表征分析表明，Enfold有效抑制无关变化，优先捕捉长期重要变化，且可以适应人为干预导致的场景突变。

Conclusion: 未来生成模型的内部推理过程可被‘内化’进当前表征，无需每步运行生成器即可高效实现预测与控制，为世界模型带来了更加高效可用的控制表征新范式。

Abstract: World generative models are typically used through what they produce: a rendered future, a video-conditioned action, or latent context computed by a costly generative branch. We argue that their more reusable asset is the computation that constructs a future. As a generator transforms a corrupted future into a coherent trajectory, its intermediate states organize appearance, spatial layout, and interaction across levels of abstraction. Can this future-generative computation be internalized in a representation inferred from the present alone? We present Enfold, which transfers this computation into a representation predicted from the current visual context and language instruction. During training, multi-level states exposed as the generator processes the observed future supervise a current-only encoder. The learned representation is fed back to condition future generation and is read by task heads without allowing task gradients to reshape the encoder. At deployment, action prediction no longer executes the generator. Across LIBERO, RoboTwin2.0, and real-robot tasks, Enfold supports strong control while reducing action latency by $3.7\times$ relative to Fast--WAM, Enfold-Flash reaches $10.1\times$. Representation analyses show that it suppresses nuisance variation and preferentially captures changes that emerge over longer horizons. When the current scene is altered by human intervention, both the generated continuation and the executed actions adapt, which is inconsistent with fixed trajectory replay. These results recast a world generator as a source of predictive control representations: its future need not be materialized at every step if its internal structure can be enfolded into the present.

</details>
